III Triplanetary

===Chinese===

III 三行星

## X Within the Red Veil / X 红幕之内
===Original===

Nevia, the home planet of the marauding spaceship, would have appeared peculiar indeed to Terrestrial senses. High in the deep red heavens a fervent blue sun poured down its flood of brilliant purplish light upon a world of water. Not a cloud was to be seen in that flaming sky, and through that dustless atmosphere the eye could see the horizon﻿—a horizon three times as distant as the one to which we are accustomed﻿—with a distinctness and clarity impossible in our Terra’s dust-filled air. As that mighty sun dropped below the horizon the sky would fill suddenly with clouds and rain would fall violently and steadily until midnight. Then the clouds would vanish as suddenly as they had come into being, the torrential downpour would cease, and through that huge world’s wonderfully transparent gaseous envelope the full glory of the firmament would be revealed. Not the firmament as we know it﻿—for that hot blue sun and Nevia, her one planet-child, were light-years distant from Old Sol and his numerous brood﻿—but a strange and glorious firmament containing few constellations familiar to Earthly eyes.
Out of the vacuum of space a fish-shaped vessel of the void﻿—the vessel that was to attack so boldly both the massed fleet of Triplanetary and Roger’s planetoid﻿—plunged into the rarefied outer atmosphere, and crimson beams of force tore shriekingly through the thin air as it braked its terrific speed. A third of the circumference of Nevia’s mighty globe was traversed before the velocity of the craft could be reduced sufficiently to make a landing possible. Then, approaching the twilight zone, the vessel dived vertically downward, and it became evident that Nevia was neither entirely aqueous nor devoid of intelligent life. For the blunt nose of the spaceship was pointing toward what was evidently a half-submerged city, a city whose buildings were flat-topped, hexagonal towers, exactly alike in size, shape, color, and material. These buildings were arranged as the cells of a honeycomb would be if each cell were separated from its neighbors by a relatively narrow channel of water, and all were built of the same white metal. Many bridges and more tubes extended through the air from building to building, and the watery “streets” teemed with swimmers, with surface craft, and with submarines.

===Chinese===

内维亚（Nevia），那艘劫掠飞船的母星，在地球人的感官里实在古怪。高悬在深红的天顶上，一颗炽烈的蓝色太阳向一个水的世界倾泻着大片紫辉。那片燃烧的天空里见不到一丝云，透过无尘的大气，肉眼可以把地平线看个分明——那地平线比我们习惯的要远上三倍——这般清晰分明，在我们这尘埃满布的泰拉大气里绝无可能。等那轮巨日一沉到地平线下，天穹会骤然布满云层，暴雨将狂暴而持续地下到午夜。而后云又像来时一样骤然散尽，倾盆大雨戛然而止，透过这个巨大世界那奇妙的透明气体外衣，满天繁星将尽显其辉煌。那不是我们熟识的星空——因为那颗炽热的蓝太阳和内维亚这颗它唯一的行星孩子，与老太阳和他那一大家子相距不知多少光年——而是一片陌生而壮丽的星空，地球人眼里熟悉的星座寥寥无几。
一艘鱼形的太空之舟破开真空——正是那艘将要同时大胆进击三行星（Triplanetary）集结舰队与罗杰小行星的船——扎入稀薄的外层大气，绯红的力量束嘶啸着撕开薄薄的空气，为它骇人的速度刹车。绕着内维亚这颗巨球跑了三分之一圈，船速才减到可以降落。而后，逼近晨昏线，那船垂直俯冲而下；至此内维亚显然既非全然水泽，也并非没有智慧生命。因为那太空船钝钝的船首正指向一座显然半没于水中的城市；城中的建筑是平顶的六角塔楼，大小、形状、颜色与材料如出一辙。这些建筑排列如蜂巢的巢室，只是每间巢室与四邻之间隔着一条颇窄的水道；全部由同一种白色金属建成。桥与更多的管道在楼宇之间凌空相连，水做的“街道”里挤满游水的人、水面舟船与潜水艇。

===Original===

The pilot, stationed immediately below the conical prow of the spaceship, peered intently through thick windows which afforded unobstructed vision in every direction. His four huge and contractile eyes were active, each operating independently in sending its own message to his peculiar but capable brain. One was watching the instruments, the others scanned narrowly the immense, swelling curve of the ship’s belly, the water upon which his vessel was to land, and the floating dock to which it was to be moored. Four hands﻿—if hands they could be called﻿—manipulated levers and wheels with infinite delicacy of touch, and with scarcely a splash the immense mass of the Nevian vessel struck the water and glided to a stop within a foot of its exact berth.
Four mooring bars dropped neatly into their sockets and the captain-pilot, after locking his controls in neutral, released his safety straps and leaped lightly from his padded bench to the floor. Scuttling across the floor and down a runway upon his four short, powerful, heavily scaled legs, he slipped smoothly into the water and flashed away, far below the surface. For Nevians are true amphibians. Their blood is cold; they use with equal comfort and efficiency gills and lungs for breathing; their scaly bodies are equally at home in the water or in the air; their broad, flat feet serve equally well for running about upon a solid surface or for driving their streamlined bodies through the water at a pace few fishes can equal.
Through the water the Nevian commander darted along, steering his course accurately by means of his short, vaned tail. Through an opening in a wall he sped and along a submarine hallway, emerging upon a broad ramp. He scurried up the incline and into an elevator which lifted him to the top of the hexagon, directly into the office of the Secretary of Commerce of all Nevia.
“Welcome, Captain Nerado!” The Secretary waved a tentacular arm and the visitor sprang lightly upon a softly cushioned bench, where he lay at ease, facing the official across his low, flat “desk.” “We congratulate you upon the success of your final trial flight. We received all your reports, even while you were traveling at ten times the velocity of light. With the last difficulties overcome, you are now ready to start?”
“We are ready,” the captain-scientist replied, soberly.

===Chinese===

驾驶员坐在太空船锥形船首的正下方，透过厚厚的、视野毫无遮拦的窗子凝神眺望。他四只巨大而能伸缩的眼睛各自忙碌，各自独立地把自己看到的消息送往他那颗奇特而管用的脑子。一只盯着仪表，其余的细细扫过船腹那道浩大鼓胀的弧线、即将降落的水面，以及要停泊的浮码头。四只手——姑且称它们为手——以无限精微的触感操纵着杠杆与轮盘；那庞大的内维亚船身几乎不带水花地触水，滑行到距泊位分毫不差的一英尺之内停住。
四根系泊棒利落地插进泊座；机长兼驾驶员把操纵锁进空挡，解开安全带，从衬垫的长凳上轻身跃下地板。他蹬着四条短促有力、披着重鳞的腿，嗒嗒地横穿地板、跑下坡道，顺滑地滑入水中，在水下深处一闪而去。内维亚人是真正的两栖类。他们的血是冷的；鳃与肺用于呼吸，同样的自在，同样的高效；他们披鳞的身体在水里在空中同样安适；宽而扁的脚，既能在坚实的表面上奔走，又能驱动他们流线型的身体在水中游出少有鱼类追得上的速度。
那位内维亚指挥官在水中一路疾冲，以短短的有舵尾巴精准把着航向。他穿过一道墙上的开口，沿一条水下长廊飞掠，冒出一道宽阔的坡道。他奔上斜坡，钻进一部电梯，被送到六角塔的顶端，径直进了全内维亚商务卿的办公室。
“欢迎，内拉多（Nerado）船长！”商务卿挥起一条触腕；来客轻身跃上一张软垫长凳，舒舒服服地伏在那里，隔着低而平的“书案”面对这位官员。“恭喜你最终试飞成功。你的报告我们全收到了，哪怕你正以十倍光速航行。最后几道难关既然都已攻克，你们现在可以启程了？”
“可以了。”这位船长兼科学家沉声答道。

===Original===

“Mechanically, the ship is as nearly perfect as our finest minds can make her. She is stocked for two years. All the iron-bearing suns within reach have been plotted. Everything is ready except the iron. Of course the Council refused to allow us any of the national supply﻿—how much were you able to purchase for us in the market?”
“Nearly ten pounds.﻿ ﻿…”
“Ten pounds! Why, the securities we left with you could not have bought two pounds, even at the price then prevailing!”
“No, but you have friends. Many of us believe in you, and have dipped into our own resources. You and your fellow scientists of the expedition have each contributed his entire personal fortune; why should not some of the rest of us also contribute, as private citizens?”
“Wonderful﻿—we thank you. Ten pounds!” The captain’s great triangular eyes glowed with an intense violet light. “At least a year of cruising. But﻿ ﻿… what if, after all, we should be wrong?”
“In that case you shall have consumed ten pounds of irreplaceable metal.” The Secretary was unmoved. “That is the viewpoint of the Council and of almost everyone else. It is not the waste of treasure they object to; it is the fact that ten pounds of iron will be forever lost.”
“A high price, truly,” the Columbus of Nevia assented. “And after all, I may be wrong.”
“You probably are wrong,” his host made startling answer. “It is practically certain﻿—it is almost a demonstrable mathematical fact﻿—that no other sun within hundreds of thousands of light-years of our own has a planet. In all probability Nevia is the only planet in the entire Universe. We are very probably the only intelligent life in the Universe. There is only one chance in numberless millions that anywhere within the cruising range of your newly perfected spaceship there may be an iron-bearing planet upon which you can effect a landing. There is a larger chance, however, that you may be able to find a small, cold, iron-bearing cosmic body﻿—small enough so that you can capture it. Although there are no mathematics by which to evaluate the probability of such an occurrence, it is upon that larger chance that some of us are staking a portion of our wealth. We expect no return whatever, but if you *should* by some miracle happen to succeed, what then?

===Chinese===

“机械上，这艘船已尽我们最优秀的头脑所能，近于完美。给养备足了两年。够得着的所有含铁恒星都已标定。万事俱备，只差铁。当然，理事会不肯拨给我们一两国库的铁——你在市场上替我们买到了多少？”
“将近十磅……”
“十磅！怎么，我们留在你那里的证券，照当时的行情连两磅也买不起！”
“买不起，可你有朋友。我们许多人信你，都掏了自己的腰包。你和你远征队的科学家同仁，人人捐出了全部家产；我们其余人里的一些，以私人公民的身份，为何就捐不得？”
“了不起——谢谢你。十磅！”船长巨大的三角眼里灼起强烈的紫光。“至少够巡航一年。可是……万一，到头来我们错了呢？”
“那样的话，你们就消耗掉了十磅无可挽回的金属。”商务卿不为所动。“这正是理事会和几乎所有其他人的看法。他们反对的不是糟蹋钱财；他们介意的是：十磅铁将永远失去。”
“高价，确实。”这位内维亚的哥伦布附和道。“况且说到底，我可能真错了。”
“你多半就是错了。”他的东道主给出惊人回答。“几乎可以断言——差不多是可证明的数学事实——方圆几十万光年之内，没有第二颗恒星拥有行星。十之八九，内维亚是全宇宙唯一的行星。我们也很可能是全宇宙唯一的智慧生命。在你那艘新近完善的飞船的巡航范围之内，存在一颗含铁行星、且容你降落其上，机会只有数百万分之一。不过，找到一颗小的、冷的、含铁的宇宙天体——小到你们能将它俘获——机会倒大一些。尽管没有哪门数学能估出这种事的概率，我们中的一些人押上的，正是这份较大的机会，连同一部分家财。我们不求任何回报；可万一、真有奇迹，你居然成功了——那又如何？

===Original===

Deep seas being made shallow, civilization extending itself over the globe, science advancing by leaps and bounds, Nevia becoming populated as she should be peopled﻿—that, my friend, is a chance well worth taking!”
The Secretary called in a group of guards, who escorted the small package of priceless metal to the spaceship. Before the massive door was sealed the friends bade each other farewell.
“… I will keep in touch with you on the ultra-wave,” the Captain concluded. “After all, I do not blame the Council for refusing to allow the other ship to go out. Ten pounds of iron will be a fearful loss to the world. If we *should* find iron, however, see to it that she loses no time in following us.”
“No fear of that! If you find iron she will set out at once, and all space will soon be full of vessels. Goodbye.”
The last opening was sealed and Nerado shot the great vessel into the air. Up and up, out beyond the last tenuous trace of atmosphere, on and on through space it flew with ever-increasing velocity until Nevia’s gigantic blue sun had been left so far behind that it became a splendid blue-white star. Then, projectors cut off to save the precious iron whose disintegration furnished them power, for week after week Captain Nerado and his venturesome crew of scientists drifted idly through the illimitable void.
There is no need to describe in detail Nerado’s tremendous voyage. Suffice it to say that he found a G-type dwarf star possessing planets﻿—not one planet only, but six﻿ ﻿… seven﻿ ﻿… eight﻿ ﻿… yes, at least nine! And most of those worlds were themselves centers of attraction around which were circling one or more worldlets! Nerado thrilled with joy as he applied a full retarding force, and every creature aboard that great vessel had to peer into a plate or through a telescope before he could believe that planets other than Nevia did in reality exist!
Velocity checked to the merest crawl, as space-speeds go, and with electromagnetic detector screens full out, the Nevian vessel crept toward our sun. Finally the detectors encountered an obstacle, a conductive substance which the patterns showed conclusively to be practically pure iron. Iron﻿—an enormous mass of it﻿—floating alone out in space!

===Chinese===

深海变成浅滩，文明铺满全球，科学一日千里，内维亚住满它应有的人口——我的朋友，这样的机会，值得一赌！”
商务卿唤来一队卫兵，护送那一小包无价的金属登船。厚重的舱门落封之前，朋友们互道珍重。
“……我会用超波（ultra-wave）同你们保持联络。”船长最后说。“毕竟，理事会不肯放另一艘船出去，我不怪他们。十磅铁对这个世界将是可怕的损失。可我们万一真找到了铁，务必叫她即刻跟上我们。”
“这何须担心！你们若找到铁，她马上启程，要不了多久，太空里就会挤满船只。再见。”
最后一道开口封死，内拉多把巨船射入空中。升，再升，冲出大气最后一缕薄痕，在太空中越飞越快，直到内维亚那颗巨大的蓝太阳被远远抛在身后，缩成一粒璀璨的蓝白星。随后，投射器关闭，以省下那宝贵的铁——正是铁的解离为他们供能——内拉多船长和他那班胆大的科学家船员，一星期又一星期，在无垠虚空里随波逐流般漂流。
内拉多那场壮阔的远航，无须细表。只消说：他找到了一颗拥有行星的G型矮星——不止一颗行星，而是六颗……七颗……八颗……不错，至少九颗！而且那些世界大半自身也是引力中心，环绕着一颗乃至多颗小世界！内拉多满怀狂喜施加全功率的减速，这艘巨船上的每一个生灵都得凑到一块底板或一支望远镜前，才敢相信内维亚之外的行星当真存在！
航速刹到太空尺度上的蜗行，电磁探测护屏（screens）尽数张开，内维亚船朝我们的太阳匍匐而来。终于，探测器撞上了障碍物，一种导电的物质，图样明明白白显示：几乎是纯铁。铁——好大一团铁——孤零零漂浮在太空中！

===Original===

Without waiting to investigate the nature, appearance, or structure of the precious mass, Nerado ordered power into the converters and drove an enormous softening field of force upon the object﻿—a force of such a nature that it would condense the metallic iron into an allotropic modification of much smaller bulk; a red, viscous, extremely dense and heavy liquid which could be stored conveniently in his tanks.
No sooner had the precious fluid been stored away than the detectors again broke into an uproar. In one direction was an enormous mass of iron, scarcely detectable; in another a great number of smaller masses; in a third an isolated mass, comparatively small in size. Space seemed to be full of iron, and Nerado drove his most powerful beam toward distant Nevia and sent an exultant message.
“We have found iron﻿—easily obtained and in unthinkable quantity﻿—not in fractions of milligrams, but in millions upon unmeasured millions of tons! Send our sister ship here at once!”
“Nerado!” The captain was called to one of the observation plates as soon as he had opened his key. “I have been investigating the mass of iron now nearest us, the small one. It is an artificial structure, a small space-boat, and there are three creatures in it﻿—monstrosities certainly, but they must possess some intelligence or they could not be navigating space.”
“What? Impossible!” exclaimed the chief explorer. “Probably, then, the other was﻿—but no matter, we had to have the iron. Bring the boat in without converting it, so that we may study at our leisure both the beings and their mechanisms,” and Nerado swung his own visiray beam into the emergency boat, seeing there the armored figures of Clio Marsden and the two Triplanetary officers.
“They are indeed intelligent,” Nerado commented, as he detected and silenced Costigan’s ultra-beam communicator. “Not, however, as intelligent as I had supposed,” he went on, after studying the peculiar creatures and their tiny spaceship more in detail. “They have immense stores of iron, yet use it for nothing other than building material. They make little and inefficient use of atomic energy. They apparently have a rudimentary knowledge of ultra-waves, but do not use them intelligently﻿—they cannot neutralize even these ordinary forces we are now employing.

===Chinese===

不待查究这团贵重物质的本性、外观或结构，内拉多便下令给换能器输送功率，将一大片软化力场压上那东西——那力场的性质，会把金属铁凝成体积小得多的同素异形体：一种红色、黏稠、密度与重量都极高的液体，正好方便地存进他的储罐。
宝贵的流体刚刚入库，探测器又炸开了锅。一个方向有一大团铁，远得几乎探不出；另一个方向是一大批较小的铁块；第三个方向有一团孤立的、个头颇小的铁。太空里似乎满是铁，内拉多把他最强的一道波束打向遥远的内维亚，发出一条扬眉吐气的讯息。
“我们找到铁了——唾手可得，数量不可想象——不是论毫克，而是几百万、几万万不计其数的吨！即刻派我们的姊妹船来！”
“内拉多！”他一开键，就被叫到一块观察底板前。“我一直在查离我们最近的那团铁，小的那团。它是人造结构，一艘小太空艇，里头有三个生物——畸形是肯定的，可他们必有些智力，不然开不了太空船。”
“什么？不可能！”首席探险家失声道。“那么先前那团多半也——罢了，铁我们横竖要的。把那艇原样收进来，别转化，好让我们从容研究那些生物和他们的机器。”内拉多把自己的视射线（visiray）束扫进那艘应急艇，看见了克利奥·马斯登（Clio Marsden）和两名三行星军官的装甲身影。
“他们确有智力。”内拉多一面说，一面探出并掐熄了科斯蒂根的超波发信机。“不过，不如我料想的那般聪明。”他把那些奇特的生物和他们小小的太空船又细看了一阵，接着说。“他们坐拥海量的铁，却只拿它当建材。原子能用得既少又笨。超波看来是懂点皮毛，用得却毫无章法——连我们眼下使的这些寻常力，他们都中和不了。

===Original===

They are of course more intelligent than the lower ganoids, or even than some of the higher fishes, but by no stretch of the imagination can they be compared to us. I am quite relieved﻿—I was afraid that in my haste I might have slain members of a highly developed race.”
The helpless boat, all her forces neutralized, was brought up close to the immense flying fish. There flaming knives of force sliced her neatly into sections and the three rigid armored figures, after being bereft of their external weapons, were brought through the airlocks and into the control room, while the pieces of their boat were stored away for future study. The Nevian scientists first analyzed the air inside the spacesuits of the Terrestrials, then carefully removed the protective coverings of the captives.
Costigan﻿—fully conscious through it all and now able to move a little, since the peculiar temporary paralysis was wearing off﻿—braced himself for he knew not what shock, but it was needless; their grotesque captors were not torturers. The air, while somewhat more dense than Earth’s and of a peculiar odor, was eminently breathable, and even though the vessel was motionless in space an almost-normal gravitation gave them a large fraction of their usual weight.
After the three had been relieved of their pistols and other articles which the Nevians thought might prove to be weapons, the strange paralysis was lifted entirely. The Earthly clothing puzzled the captors immensely, but so strenuous were the objections raised to its removal that they did not press the point, but fell back to study their find in detail.
Then faced each other the representatives of the civilizations of two widely separated solar systems. The Nevians studied the human beings with interest and curiosity blended largely with loathing and repulsion; the three Terrestrials regarded the unmoving, expressionless “faces”﻿—if those coned heads could be said to possess such thing﻿—with horror and disgust, as well as with other emotions, each according to his type and training. For to human eyes the Nevian is a fearful thing. Even today there are few Terrestrials﻿—or Solarians, for that matter﻿—who can look at a Nevian, eye to eye, without feeling a creeping of the skin and experiencing a “gone” sensation in the pit of the stomach.

===Chinese===

他们当然比下等的硬鳞鱼聪明些，甚至比某些高等鱼类也聪明些，可任凭怎么想象，也同我们相提并论不得。我大大松了口气——我还怕自己一时性急，杀了一个高度发达种族的成员呢。”
那艘束手无策的小艇，全部力量尽遭中和，被拖到那条巨大的飞鱼跟前。灼如刀刃的力量束把她利落地切成几段；三具僵直的装甲身影，被卸去外部武器后，穿过气闸带进操纵室；他们小艇的碎块则收拢入库，留待日后研究。内维亚的科学家先分析了地球人太空服里的空气，再小心剥去俘虏的防护外壳。
科斯蒂根——全程神志清醒，此刻已能稍稍动弹，那种奇特的暂时麻痹正在消退——攥紧全身准备迎接不知什么样的冲击，却是多虑；这些怪模怪样的掳主并非用刑之人。空气比地球的略稠，带着股怪味，却完全可吸；船虽静止在太空中，一股近乎正常的重力仍给了他们平常体重的一大部分。
三人被收去手枪和其余内维亚人疑为武器的东西之后，那怪异的麻痹彻底解除。地球人的衣服叫掳主们大惑不解，可掀掉它们遭到的抗议太过激烈，他们便不再坚持，退而细细研究他们的猎获。
于是，两个相隔遥远的太阳系的文明代表，面对面了。内维亚人打量这几个人类，兴趣与好奇里掺着大半的憎嫌与恶感；三个地球人望着那一张张纹丝不动、毫无表情的“脸”——倘若那几颗锥头当得起“脸”这个字眼——满心恐怖与厌恶，另掺着些别的情绪，各随各的性子与教养。因为在人类眼里，内维亚人是可怖之物。即便到今天，能同内维亚人对视而皮肤不起一阵发麻、胃里不泛一阵发空的地球人——或者说太阳系人——也为数寥寥。

===Original===

The horny, wrinkled, drought-resisting Martian, whom we all know and rather like, is a hideous being indeed. The bat-eyed, colorless, hairless, practically skinless Venerian is worse. But they both are, after all, remote cousins of Terra’s humanity, and we get along with them quite well whenever we are compelled to visit Mars or Venus. But the Nevians﻿—
The horizontal, flat, fish-like body is not so bad, even supported as it is by four short, powerful, scaly, flat-footed legs; and terminating as it does in the weird, four-vaned tail. The neck, even, is endurable, although it is long and flexible, heavily scaled, and is carried in whatever eye-wringing loops or curves the owner considers most convenient or ornamental at the time. Even the smell of a Nevian﻿—a malodorous reek of overripe fish﻿—does in time become tolerable, especially if sufficiently disguised with creosote, which purely Terrestrial chemical is the most highly prized perfume of Nevia. But the head! It is that member that makes the Nevian so appalling to Earthly eyes, for it is a thing utterly foreign to all Solarian history or experience. As most Tellurians already know, it is fundamentally a massive cone, covered with scales, based spearhead-like upon the neck. Four great sea-green, triangular eyes are spaced equidistant from each other about halfway up the cone. The pupils are contractile at will, like the eyes of the cat, permitting the Nevian to see equally well in any ordinary extreme of light or darkness. Immediately below each eye springs out a long, jointless, boneless, tentacular arm; an arm which at its extremity divides into eight delicate and sensitive, but very strong, “fingers.” Below each arm is a mouth: a beaked, needle-tusked orifice of dire potentialities. Finally, under the overhanging edge of the cone-shaped head are the delicately-frilled organs which serve either as gills or as nostrils and lungs, as may be desired. To other Nevians the eyes and other features are highly expressive, but to us they appear utterly cold and unmoving. Terrestrial senses can detect no changes of expression in a Nevian’s “face.” Such were the frightful beings at whom the three prisoners stared with sinking hearts.
But if we human beings have always considered Nevians grotesque and repulsive, the feeling has always been mutual.

===Chinese===

火星人那副角质多皱、耐旱的尊容，咱们都认得，也还相当喜欢，其实已算丑得可以。金星人那副蝙蝠眼、无色无毛、几乎无皮的模样更糟。可他们好歹是泰拉人类的远房表亲，我们去火星金星公干，同他们处得也还算好。可内维亚人——
那横卧的、扁平的鱼形身子，倒还不算太糟，哪怕它架在四条短促有力、披鳞扁足的腿上，末端又拖着一条四舵的怪尾。脖子也还受得住，虽然它又长又软、鳞甲厚重，还要随着主人的方便或审美，拧成各种叫人眼疼的环与弯。就连内维亚人的气味——一股烂熟鱼的恶臭——天长日久也还受得下来，尤其若用杂酚油重重遮过——这种纯地球化学品，正是内维亚身价最高的香水。可那颗头！让内维亚人在地球人眼里如此骇人的，正是这件东西，因为它全然不见于太阳系的历史与经验。大多数特卢斯人都已知道，它根本上是一枚巨大的鳞甲圆锥，矛尖似的基座安在脖子上。四只大海绿色的三角眼睛，绕锥体等距排布在半腰。瞳孔随意愿收缩，如同猫眼，任凭寻常明暗的极端，内维亚人都一样看得清楚。每只眼睛的正下方，探出一条长长的、无节无骨的触腕；腕端分成八根纤细灵敏却十分有力的“指”。每条腕下是一张嘴：一张带喙、针状獠牙的口，凶险莫测。最后，锥形头前伸的檐下，是那几片精巧镶边的器官，要用作鳃还是鼻与肺，悉听尊便。在内维亚同族眼里，这些眼睛和其余部件表情丰富；在我们看来，却全然冰冷僵死。地球人的感官，在内维亚人的“脸”上察觉不出任何表情变化。三个囚徒心一寸寸下沉，直勾勾望着的，便是这样一些可怖的生灵。
可如果说我们人类一向觉得内维亚人怪诞可憎，这感觉从来是相互的。

===Original===

For those “monstrous” beings are a highly intelligent and extremely sensitive race, and our﻿—to us﻿—trim and graceful human forms seem to them the very quintessence of malformation and hideousness.
“Good Heavens, Conway!” Clio exclaimed, shrinking against Costigan as his left arm flashed around her. “What horrible monstrosities! And they can’t talk﻿—not one of them has made a sound﻿—suppose they can be deaf and dumb?”
But at the same time Nerado was addressing his fellows.
“What hideous, deformed creatures they are! Truly a low form of life, even though they do possess some intelligence. They cannot talk, and have made no signs of having heard our words to them﻿—do you suppose that they communicate by sight? That those weird contortions of their peculiarly placed organs serve as speech?”
Thus both sides, neither realizing that the other had spoken. For the Nevian voice is pitched so high that the lowest note audible to them is far above our limit of hearing. The shrillest note of a Terrestrial piccolo is to them so profoundly low that it cannot be heard.
“We have much to do.” Nerado turned away from the captives. “We must postpone further study of the specimens until we have taken aboard a full cargo of the iron which is so plentiful here.”
“What shall we do with them, sir?” asked one of the Nevian officers. “Lock them in one of the storage rooms?”
“Oh, no! They might die there, and we must by all means keep them in good condition, to be studied most carefully by the fellows of the College of Science. What a commotion there will be when we bring in this group of strange creatures, living proof that there are other suns possessing planets; planets which are supporting organic and intelligent life! You may put them in three communicating rooms, say in the fourth section﻿—they will undoubtedly require light and exercise. Lock all the exits, of course, but it would be best to leave the doors between the rooms unlocked, so that they can be together or apart, as they choose. Since the smallest one, the female, stays so close to the larger male, it may be that they are mates. But since we know nothing of their habits or customs, it will be best to give them all possible freedom compatible with safety.”
Nerado turned back to his instruments and three of the frightful crew came up to the human beings.

===Chinese===

因为那些“畸形”的生物，是一个智力极高、感觉极敏的种族；而我们这副在我们看来匀称优雅的人形，在他们眼里恰是残缺与丑恶的极致。
“老天爷，康韦！”克利奥惊呼，往科斯蒂根身上缩，他的左臂闪电般拢住她。“多可怕的怪物！他们又不会说话——一个都没出过声——你说，会不会又聋又哑？”
而与此同时，内拉多也正对他的同僚们说话。
“多丑陋、多畸形的生物！确实是低等生命，纵然多少有点智力。他们不会说话，我们对他们讲话也不见丝毫听懂的迹象——你说，他们会不会靠视觉交流？他们那些位置古怪的器官抽来扭去，会不会就是言语？”
双方就这样各说各的，谁也不知对方开了口。因为内维亚人的嗓音音高极高，他们能听见的最低音，也远在我们听觉的极限之上。地球短笛最尖的音，在他们听来低得深不可闻。
“我们事多。”内拉多从俘虏们身边转过身去。“得先把这遍地皆是的好铁装满一船，标本的研究且往后放放。”
“怎么处置他们，长官？”一名内维亚军官问。“锁进哪间库房？”
“哦，不！他们会死在里头的，而我们务必把他们保养得好好的，好教理学院的同仁们细细研究。等我们把这一批怪生物带回去——其他恒星也拥有行星、行星上还滋养着有机智慧生命的活证——那该是怎样一场轰动！把他们安置在三间相通的屋子里，就第四区吧——他们想必需要光亮和活动。当然，一切出口统统上锁，但几间屋之间的门最好别锁，让他们聚散随愿。那最小的一只，雌的，同那只大些的雄的形影不离，说不定是一对。反正他们的习性风俗我们一概不知，最稳妥的做法，是在安全无虞的前提下给他们一切可能的自由。”
内拉多转回他的仪表，三个可怖的船员朝人类走来。

===Original===

One walked away, waving a couple of arms in an unmistakable signal that the prisoners were to follow him. The three obediently set out after him, the other two guards falling behind.
“Now’s our best chance!” Costigan muttered, as they passed through a low doorway and entered a narrow corridor. “Watch that one ahead of you, Clio﻿—hold him for a second if you can. Bradley, you and I will take the two behind us﻿—now!”
Costigan stooped and whirled. Seizing a cable-like arm, he pulled the outlandish head down, the while the full power of his mighty right leg drove a heavy service boot into the place where scaly neck and head joined. The Nevian fell, and instantly Costigan leaped at the leader, ahead of the girl. Leaped; but dropped to the floor, again paralyzed. For the Nevian leader had been alert, his four eyes covering the entire circle of vision, and he had acted rapidly. Not in time to stop Costigan’s first berserk attack﻿—the First Officer’s reactions were practically instantaneous and he moved fast﻿—but in time to retain command of the situation. Another Nevian appeared, and while the stricken guard was recovering, all four arms wrapped tightly around his convulsively looping, writhing neck, the three helpless Terrestrials were lifted into the air and carried bodily into the quarters to which Nerado had assigned them. Not until they had been placed upon cushions in the middle room and the heavy metal doors had been locked upon them did they again find themselves able to use arms or legs.
“Well, that’s another round we lose,” Costigan commented, cheerfully. “A guy can’t mix it very well when he can neither kick, strike, nor bite. I expected those lizards to rough me up then, but they didn’t.”
“They don’t want to hurt us. They want to take us home with them, wherever that is, as curiosities, like wild animals or something,” decided the girl, shrewdly. “They’re pretty bad, of course, but I like them a lot better than I do Roger and his robots, anyway.”
“I think you have the right idea, Miss Marsden,” Bradley rumbled. “That’s it, exactly. I feel like a bear in a cage. I should think you’d feel worse than ever. What chance has an animal of escaping from a menagerie?”
“These animals, lots. I’m feeling better and better all the time,” Clio declared, and her serene bearing bore out her words.

===Chinese===

一个走开去，挥舞着一两条腕，示意再明白不过：囚徒跟他走。三人顺从地上路，另外两个卫兵坠在后面。
“眼下就是最好的机会！”穿过一道矮门、进了一条窄廊时，科斯蒂根低声道。“盯住你前面那个，克利奥——办得到就拖他一秒。布拉德利，咱俩收拾后面两个——上！”
科斯蒂根一躬身旋过去，攥住一条缆索般的手臂，把那颗怪头拽下来，同时右腿倾尽全力，一只沉重的军靴直踹进鳞颈与头颅的接缝。那内维亚人倒了；科斯蒂根即刻扑向姑娘前方的那名领队。扑是扑出去了；人却瘫回地板，又麻痹了。原来那内维亚领队早有戒备，四只眼照管着整圈视野，出手也快。虽没能拦下科斯蒂根头一阵疯魔般的突袭——大副的反应几乎就在瞬间，动得也快——却来得及把局面攥在手里。又一名内维亚人赶到；挨了那一下的卫兵还在缓神，四条手臂死死绞住自己那正痉挛成环、拼命扭动的脖子；三个动弹不得的地球人被凌空提起，整个儿抱进内拉多指派的住处。直到他们被安放在当中那间的软垫上、厚重的金属门在他们身后落锁，他们才重新使得动手脚。
“得，又输一个回合。”科斯蒂根倒乐呵呵。“一个人既踢不得、打不得、又咬不得，还怎么近身格斗。我本以为这几条蜥蜴要收拾我一顿，倒没有。”
“他们不想伤我们。他们要把我们带回家去，不管家在哪儿，当稀罕物，跟野兽什么的似的。”姑娘冰雪聪明，一语道破。“他们当然够坏的，可比起罗杰和他那帮机器人，我已经喜欢他们多了。”
“我看你说到点子上了，马斯登小姐。”布拉德利瓮声道。“正是这么回事。我感觉自己像笼子里的熊。倒是你，照理该更难受才是。动物园里的动物，跑掉的机会有多大？”
“咱们这几只，机会大得很。我越待越踏实。”克利奥宣布，她那副安详的神气正为这话作保。

===Original===

“You two got us out of that horrible place of Roger’s, and I’m pretty sure that you will get us away from here, somehow or other. They may think we’re stupid animals, but before you two and the Triplanetary Patrol and the Service get done with them they’ll have another think coming.”
“That’s the old fight, Clio!” cheered Costigan. “I haven’t got it figured out as close as you have, but I get about the same answer. These four-legged fish carry considerably heavier stuff than Roger did, I’m thinking; but they’ll be up against something themselves pretty quick that is *no* lightweight, believe me!”
“Do you know something, or are you just whistling in the dark?” Bradley demanded.
“I know a little; not much. Engineering and Research have been working on a new ship for a long time; a ship to travel so much faster than light that it can go anywhere in the Galaxy and back in a month or so. New sub-ether drive, new atomic power, new armament, new everything. Only bad thing about it is that it doesn’t work so good yet﻿—it’s fuller of bugs than a Venerian’s kitchen. It has blown up five times that I know of, and has killed twenty-nine men. But when they get it licked they’ll *have something*!”
“When, or if?” asked Bradley, pessimistically.
“I said *when*!” snapped Costigan, his voice cutting. “When the Service goes after anything they get it, and when they get it it *stays*.﻿ ﻿…” He broke off abruptly and his voice lost its edge. “Sorry. Didn’t mean to get high, but I think we’ll have help, if we can keep our heads up a while. And it looks good﻿—these are first-class cages they’ve given us. All the comforts of home, even to lookout plates. Let’s see what’s going on, shall we?”
After some experimenting with the unfamiliar controls Costigan learned how to operate the Nevian visiray, and upon the plate they saw the Cone of Battle hurling itself toward Roger’s planetoid. They saw the pirate fleet rush out to do battle with Triplanetary’s massed forces, and with bated breath they watched every maneuver of that epic battle to its savagely sacrificial end. And that same battle was being watched, also with the most intense interest, by the Nevians in their control room.
“It is indeed a bloodthirsty combat,” mused Nerado at his observation plate.

===Chinese===

“你们俩既然能把我们从罗杰那个鬼地方弄出来，我就不信没法子把咱们从这里弄出去，反正总有法子。他们只当我们是蠢牲口；可等你们两位，加上三行星巡逻队和整个服务团跟他们算完账，有他们再想的。”
“这才是那个倔丫头，克利奥！”科斯蒂根给她打气。“我盘算得没你那么细，可答案也差不多。这几条四条腿的鱼，家伙比罗杰的沉得多，我看；可他们马上也要撞上个决非轻量级的对手了，信我！”
“你是知道点内情，还是黑地里吹口哨壮胆？”布拉德利质问。
“知道一点；不多。工程和研究两部造一艘新船，造了很久了；一艘快过光速太多的船，快到一个月上下就能逛遍银河再打来回。新亚以太驱动，新原子能，新武备，全新的一切。唯一的毛病是眼下还不太好使——毛病比金星人的厨房还多。就我所知已经炸过五回，死了二十九个人。可等他们把这颗硬骨头啻下来，那可就真有家伙了！”
“‘等’，还是‘如果’？”布拉德利悲观地问。
“我说的是等！”科斯蒂根嗓音一厉。“服务团要什么就拿到什么；拿到手，就永远攥住。……”他猛地收住，话锋软了下来。“对不住，不该来火。可我想，只要咱们把脑袋再举一阵子，救兵就到。而且眼下光景不坏——他们给的可是头等牢笼。居家样样舒适，连瞭望底板都有。瞧瞧外头在干什么，好不好？”
摆弄了一阵那套陌生的控制器，科斯蒂根学会了操纵内维亚人的视射线；底板上，他们看见战斗锥阵正朝罗杰的小行星猛掷过去。他们看见海盗舰队冲出去迎战三行星的集结大军，屏住呼吸看完了那场史诗之战的每一步调度，直到它惨烈自我牺牲的终局。而同一场战斗，也在内维亚人的操纵室里，被以同样炽烈的兴味注视着。
“这确乎是一场嗜血的厮杀。”内拉多在观察底板前沉吟。

===Original===

“And it is peculiar﻿—or rather, probably only to be expected from a race of such a low stage of development﻿—that they employ only ether-borne forces. Warfare seems universal among primitive types﻿—indeed, it is not so long ago that our own cities, few in number though they are, ceased fighting each other and combined against the semicivilized fishes of the greater deeps.”
He fell silent, and for many minutes watched the furious battle between the two navies of the void. That conflict ended, he watched the Triplanetary fleet reform its battle cone and rush upon the planetoid.
“Destruction, always destruction,” he sighed, adjusting his power switches. “Since they are bent upon mutual destruction I can see no purpose in refraining from destroying all of them. We need the iron, and they are a useless race.”
He launched his softening, converting field of dull red energy. Vast as that field was, it could not encompass the whole fleet, but half of the lip of the gigantic cone soon disappeared, its component vessels subsiding into a sluggishly flowing stream of allotropic iron. The fleet, abandoning its attack upon the planetoid, swung its cone around, to bring the flame-erupting axis to bear upon the formless something dimly perceptible to the ultra-vision of Samms’ observers. Furiously the gigantic composite beam of the massed fleet was hurled, nor was it alone.
For Gharlane had known, ever since the easy escape of his human prisoners, that something was occurring which was completely beyond his experience, although not beyond his theoretical knowledge. He had found the sub-ether closed; he had been unable to make his sub-ethereal weapons operative against either the three captives or the war-vessels of the Triplanetary Patrol. Now, however, he could work in the sub-ethereal murk of the newcomers; a light trial showed him that if he so wished he could use sub-ethereal offenses against them. What was the real meaning of those facts?
He had become convinced that those three persons were no more human than was Roger himself. Who or what was activating them? It was definitely not Eddorian workmanship; no Eddorian would have developed those particular techniques, nor could possibly have developed them without his knowledge. What, then?

===Chinese===

“而且古怪——或者说，对这样一个发展阶段低下的种族，多半意料之中——他们用的尽是以太之力。战争在原始类型里似乎无处不在——其实也不算太久以前，我们自己那几座城，尽管数目寥寥，才刚刚罢了互相攻伐、联手对付大深槽里那些半文明的鱼类。”
他住了口，一连许多分钟盯着两支太空舰队恶战。那边战罢，他又看着三行星舰队重结战斗锥，扑向小行星。
“毁灭，总是毁灭。”他叹道，调着功率开关。“他们既一心互相毁灭，我看不出有什么理由不把他们统统毁灭。我们要的是铁，而这个种族毫无用处。”
他放出了那片软化的、转化的暗红能量场。那力场虽巨，罩不住整支舰队，可巨锥的唇边不久便消失了一半，组成它的舰只，纷纷瘫作一股缓缓流淌的同素异形铁流。舰队放弃了攻袭小行星，把锥阵整个旋了过来，让喷焰的中轴对准那团只有萨姆斯观察员们的超视觉才隐约可辨的无形之物。集结舰群那道巨大的合成波束狂怒掷出，而且掷出的不止它一个。
因为加兰（Gharlane）早自那三个人类俘虏轻易脱逃之时就明白：正有桩事情发生，全然超出他的经验——虽不出他的理论知识。他发现亚以太被封闭了；无论对那三名俘虏，还是对三行星巡逻队的战舰，他的亚以太兵器都打不响。可如今，在这些新来者造成的亚以太迷雾里，他却使得上劲了；一次轻轻的试探便告诉他：只要他愿意，他可以对他们动用亚以太攻势。这些事实，究竟说明什么？
他已深信，那三个人同罗杰本人一样，绝非人类。是谁、是什么在驱动他们？那绝不是埃多尔（Eddore）人的手笔；任何埃多尔人都造不出那些特定的技术，也不可能瞒着他造出来。那么，是什么？

===Original===

To do what had been done necessitated the existence of a race as old and as capable as the Eddorians, but of an entirely different nature; and, according to Eddore’s vast Information Center, no such race existed or ever had existed.
Those visitors, possessing mechanisms supposedly known only to the science of Eddore, would also be expected to possess the mental powers which had been exhibited. Were they recent arrivals from some other space-time continuum? Probably not﻿—Eddorian surveys had found no trace of any such life in any reachable plenum. Since it would be utterly fantastic to postulate the unheralded appearance of two such races at practically the same moment, the conclusion seemed unavoidable that these as yet unknown beings were the protectors﻿—the activators, rather﻿—of the two Triplanetary officers and the woman. This view was supported by the fact that while the strangers had attacked Triplanetary’s fleet and had killed thousands of Triplanetary’s men, they had actually rescued those three supposedly human beings. The planetoid, then would be attacked next. Very well, he would join Triplanetary in attacking them﻿—with weapons no more dangerous to them than Triplanetary’s own﻿—the while preparing his real attack, which would come later. Roger issued orders; and waited; and thought more and more intensely upon one point which remained obscure﻿—why, when the strangers themselves destroyed Triplanetary’s fleet, had Roger been unable to use his most potent weapons against that fleet?
Thus, then, for the first time in Triplanetary’s history, the forces of law and order joined hands with those of piracy and banditry against a common foe. Rods, beams, planes, and stilettos of unbearable energy the doomed fleet launched, in addition to its terrifically destructive main beam: Roger hurled every material weapon at his command. But bombs, high-explosive shells, even the ultra-deadly atomic torpedoes, alike were ineffective; alike simply vanished in the redly murky veil of nothingness. And the fleet was being melted. In quick succession the vessels flamed red, shrank together, gave out their air, and merged their component iron into the intensely crimson, sullenly viscous stream which was flowing through the impenetrable veil against which both Triplanetarians and pirates were directing their terrific offense.

===Chinese===

要做成了这些事，必得存在一个同埃多尔人一样古老、一样能干的种族，本性却截然不同；而据埃多尔那座浩大的信息中心，这样的种族不曾存在，也从未存在过。
那些来客既然拥有理应唯埃多尔科学才晓得的神机，想必也拥有他们展示过的那些心智之力。他们是从别的时空连续体新来的？多半不是——埃多尔的勘察，在任何够得着的充实体里都不曾寻见这类生命的踪迹。既然假定两个这般种族几乎在同一时刻不打招呼地双双现身，未免荒诞绝伦，结论便似乎无可回避：这些尚且无名的生灵，正是那两名三行星军官与那个女人的保护者——不如说，驱动者。这一看法还得到一个事实的支持：陌生人虽袭击了三行星的舰队、杀了成千上万三行星的人，却实实在在救下了那三个所谓的人类。那么，下一个挨打的就是小行星了。很好；他就同三行星联手打他们——用的兵器，对那些人而言不比三行星自己的更危险——同时备下他真正的攻势，那要等以后。罗杰下达了命令；静候；同时越来越狠地琢磨着一个仍未明了的疑点——为何当陌生人自己动手毁灭三行星舰队时，罗杰竟无法把自己最厉的兵器打向那支舰队？
于是，在三行星的历史上，法律与秩序的力量第一次同海盗与匪帮联手，共御强敌。力棒、光束、力面与难以承受的能量短剑，这支注定覆灭的舰队尽数掷出，外加那道毁灭性的主巨束：罗杰把麾下每一件实体兵器全数掷出。可炸弹、高爆弹，乃至那超致命的原子鱼雷，一概无效；一概在那一团红浊的虚无之幕里凭空消失。舰队在熔化。一艘接一艘，舰身烧红、瘪缩、泄尽空气，把各自的铁并入那股殷红灼目、黏稠沉滞的洪流；洪流正穿透那道不透之幕，而三行星人与海盗双方的猛烈攻势，都正对着那道幕倾泻。

===Original===

The last vessel of the attacking cone having been converted and the resulting metal stored away, the Nevians﻿—as Roger had anticipated﻿—turned their attention toward the planetoid. But that structure was no feeble warship. It had been designed by, and built under the personal supervision of, Gharlane of Eddore. It was powered, equipped, and armed to meet any emergency which Gharlane’s tremendous mind had been able to envision. Its entire bulk was protected by the shield whose qualities had so surprised Costigan; a shield far more effective than any Tellurian scientist or engineer would have believed possible.
The voracious converting beam of the Nevians, below the level of the ether though it was, struck that shield and rebounded; defeated and futile. Struck again, again rebounded; then struck and clung hungrily, licking out over that impermeable surface in darting tongues of flame as the surprised Nerado doubled and then quadrupled his power. Fiercer and fiercer the Nevian flood of force drove in. The whole immense globe of the planetoid became one scintillant ball of raw, red energy; but still the pirates’ shield remained intact.
Gray Roger sat coldly motionless at his great desk, the top of which was now swung up to become a panel of massed and tiered instruments and controls. He could carry this load forever﻿—but unless he was very wrong, this load would change shortly. What then? The essence that was Gharlane could not be killed﻿—could not even be hurt﻿—by any physical, chemical, or nuclear force. Should he stay with the planetoid to its end, and thus perforce return to Eddore with no material evidence whatever? He would not. Too much remained undone. Any report based upon his present information could be neither complete nor conclusive, and reports submitted by Gharlane of Eddore to the coldly cynical and ruthlessly analytical Innermost Circle had always been and always would be both.
It was a fact that there existed at least one non-Eddorian mind which was the equal of his own. If one, there would be a race of such minds. The thought was galling; but to deny the existence of a fact would be the essence of stupidity. Since power of mind was a function of time, that race must be of approximately the same age as his own. Therefore the Eddorian Information Center, which by the inference of its completeness denied the existence of such a race, was wrong.

===Chinese===

进攻锥阵的最后一艘舰也化了、存的铁也存了，内维亚人——正如罗杰所料——把注意力转向小行星。可这座构造物不是什么单薄的战舰。它由埃多尔的加兰亲自设计、亲自监督建造；加兰那颗伟岸头脑所能想见的一切变局，它都以相应的动力、设备与武备迎候。它的整个庞躯护着那面曾令科斯蒂根大为惊异的盾；那面盾之有效，远非任何特卢斯科学家或工程师所能相信。
内维亚人那道贪婪的转化束，虽然行在以太层之下，打上那面盾却只有反弹；败退，徒劳。再打，再弹；继而打上去，贪婪地贴住不放，火舌在那不透的表面上乱舔——惊异的内拉多把功率加了一倍，又翻了两番。内维亚人的力之洪流越灌越凶。小行星整个浩大的球体化作一团刺目的生红能量；可海盗们的盾，依然完好。
灰色罗杰（Gray Roger）冷冷地端坐在他的大书案前，案面此时已经翻起，成了一面层层密布仪表与操控器的操控板。这份负载他扛得起，扛多久都行——可除非他大错特错，这负载很快就要变。那时怎么办？加兰的那个本体，任何物理、化学或核子的力量都杀不死——连伤都伤不得。他该守着小行星到终局，从而不得不空着两手、不带半点实证地回埃多尔么？他不干。没做完的事还太多。基于目前情报写就的任何报告，既不可能完整，也不可能定论；而埃多尔的加兰呈交给那个冷面冷心、分析无情的最高内圈的报告，从来是、也永远必须是既完整又定论。
事实是，至少存在一个非埃多尔的心智，堪与他自己的匹敌。有一个，就会有一族这样的心智。这念头叫人如鲠在喉；可否认一个事实的存在，是愚蠢的极致。既然心智之力是时间的函数，那个种族必与他自己的大约同龄。因此，埃多尔的信息中心——凭它自诩的完备，一口否认这样的种族存在——错了。

===Original===

It was not complete.
Why was it not complete? The only possible reason for two such races remaining unaware of the existence of each other would be the deliberate intent of one of them. Therefore, at some time in the past, the two races had been in contact for at least an instant of time. All Eddorian knowledge of that meeting had been suppressed and no more contacts had been allowed to occur.
The conclusion reached by Gharlane was a disturbing thing indeed; but, being an Eddorian, he faced it squarely. He did not have to wonder how such a suppression could have been accomplished﻿—he knew. He also knew that his own mind contained everything known to his every ancestor since the first Eddorian was: the probability was exceedingly great that if any such contact had ever been made his mind would still contain at least some information concerning it, however carefully suppressed that knowledge had been.
He thought. Back﻿ ﻿… back﻿ ﻿… farther back﻿ ﻿… farther still.﻿ ﻿…
And as he thought, an interfering force began to pluck at him; as though palpable tongs were pulling out of line the mental probe with which he was exploring the hitherto unplumbed recesses of his mind.
“Ah﻿ ﻿… so you do not want me to remember?” Roger asked aloud, with no change in any lineament of his hard, gray face. “I wonder﻿ ﻿… do you really believe that you can keep me from remembering? I must abandon this search for the moment, but rest assured that I shall finish it very shortly.”
---
“Here is the analysis of his screen, sir.” A Nevian computer handed his chief a sheet of metal, bearing rows of symbols.
“Ah, a polycyclic﻿ ﻿… complete coverage﻿ ﻿… a screen of that type was scarcely to have been expected from such a low form of life,” Nerado commented, and began to adjust dials and controls.
As he did so the character of the clinging mantle of force changed. From red it flamed quickly through the spectrum, became unbearably violet, then disappeared; and as it disappeared the shielding wall began to give way. It did not cave in abruptly, but softened locally, sagging into a peculiar grouping of valleys and ridges﻿—contesting stubbornly every inch of position lost.
Roger experimented briefly with inertialessness. No use. As he had expected, they were prepared for that. He summoned a few of the ablest of his scientist-slaves and issued instructions.

===Chinese===

它并不完备。
它为何不完备？两个这样的种族彼此不知对方存在，唯一可能的原因，是其中一方蓄意为之。因此，在过去某个时刻，两个种族至少有过一瞬的接触。埃多尔关于那次会面的全部知识，被人抹去了；此后再不容第二次接触发生。
加兰得出的这个结论，实在是骇人；可他到底是埃多尔人，直直面对。他不必琢磨这样的封锁如何办得到——他知道。他也知道，自第一个埃多尔人诞生以来，他每一位祖先所知的一切，他这颗头脑里应有尽有：倘若那样的接触真的发生过，任凭封得再严，他的头脑里极大概率仍会残留至少一点相关信息。
他想。往回……往回……再往回……还要往回。……
而就在他想的时候，一股干扰之力开始拉扯他；仿佛有一把有形的铁钳，正把他那支探入自己头脑幽渊的心智探针，硬生生拽出准线。
“啊……原来你们不愿我想起来？”罗杰朗声发问，那张坚硬的灰脸上不见一丝纹路挪动。“我倒纳闷……你们当真以为拦得住我？此刻我且放下这桩搜索，但放一百个心，不久我必查它个水落石出。”
---
“这是他护屏的分析，长官。”一名内维亚计算员递给长官一张金属片，上面排满符号。
“啊，多环式……全域覆盖……这样低等的生命形态竟造得出这类护屏，殊难逆料。”内拉多评道，随即动手调起度盘与操控器。
他调着，那层紧贴不放的力幔随之变样。由红而快快烧穿光谱，紫得叫人无法承受，随即消失；它一消失，护壁开始让步。不是轰然塌陷，而是各处软化，下陷成一组古怪的谷与脊——每让一寸，都要顽抗到底。
罗杰略试了试无惯态。没用。一如他所料，对方早有防备。他唤来几名最能干的科学家奴隶，下达指示。

===Original===

For minutes a host of robots toiled mightily, then a portion of the shield bulged out and became a tube extending beyond the attacking layers of force; a tube from which there erupted a beam of violence incredible. A beam behind which was every erg of energy that the gigantic mechanisms of the planetoid could yield. A beam that tore a hole through the redly impenetrable Nevian field and hurled itself upon the inner screen of the fish-shaped cruiser in frenzied incandescence. And was there, or was there not, a lesser eruption upon the other side﻿—an almost imperceptible flash, as though something had shot from the doomed planetoid out into space?
Nerado’s neck writhed convulsively as his tortured drivers whined and shrieked at the terrific overload; but Roger’s effort was far too intense to be long maintained. Generator after generator burned out, the defensive screen collapsed, and the red converter beam attacked voraciously the unresisting metal of those prodigious walls. Soon there was a terrific explosion as the pent-up air of the planetoid broke through its weakening container, and the sluggish river of allotropic iron flowed in an ever larger stream, ever faster.
“It is well that we had an unlimited supply of iron.” Nerado almost tied a knot in his neck as he spoke in huge relief. “With but the seven pounds remaining of our original supply, I fear that it would have been difficult to parry that last thrust.”
“Difficult?” asked the second in command. “We would now be free atoms in space. But what shall I do with this iron? Our reservoirs will not hold more than half of it. And how about that one ship which remains untouched?”
“Jettison enough supplies from the lower holds to make room for this lot. As for that one ship, let it go. We will be overloaded as it is, and it is of the utmost importance that we get back to Nevia as soon as possible.”
This, if Gharlane could have heard it, would have answered his question. All Arisia knew that it was *necessary* for the camera-ship to survive. The Nevians were interested only in iron; but the Eddorian, being a perfectionist, would not have been satisfied with anything less than the complete destruction of every vessel of Triplanetary’s fleet.
The Nevian spaceship moved away, sluggishly now because of its prodigious load.

===Chinese===

一大群机器人奋力劳作了几分钟；随后，盾面鼓出一块，延成一根管子，探出攻击力层之外；管口喷出一道难以置信其狂暴的束。那道束背后，是小行星那些巨大机构所能交出的每一尔格能量。那道束，在红色不可透入的内维亚力场上撕开一个洞，狂炽地砸在鱼形巡洋舰的内层护屏上。而另一侧——是、还是不是——也有一记小小的喷发？一次几不可察的闪光，仿佛有什么东西，从那座注定的小行星上射进了太空？
内拉多的脖子痉挛般扭动，他那些受苦的驱动器在骇人的超载下哀鸣尖啸；可罗杰这一击太烈，撑不长久。发电机一台接一台烧毁，防御护屏垮塌，红色的转化束贪婪地噬上那两壁高墙毫不抵抗的金属。不久便是一声天崩地裂的爆炸——小行星憋住的空气冲破了越绷越弱的容器——而同素异形铁那条黏稠的大河，越流越宽，越流越急。
“幸亏我们的铁备得无限。”内拉多如释重负，说话时脖子差点打成一个结。“若只剩原先剩下的那七磅，方才那一记，我怕是招架不住。”
“何止招架不住？”副手反问。“我们此刻已是太空里的一把自由原子了。只是这些铁怎么处置？我们的储罐至多装得下一半。还有那条完好无损的船，又怎么办？”
“把下舱的给养抛掉一批，腾地方装这批。至于那条船，随它去。我们眼下已经超载，尽早赶回内维亚才是头等大事。”
这话，加兰若是听见，正答了他那一问。整个阿里西亚（Arisia）都知道：摄影船必须活下来。内维亚人只惦记铁；可那位埃多尔人是完美主义者，三行星舰队的每一艘船，不尽数毁灭，他不会甘心。
内维亚太空船挪开了，如今它负载骇人，行动迟缓。

===Original===

In their quarters in the fourth section the three Terrestrials, who had watched with strained attention the downfall and absorption of the planetoid, stared at each other with drawn faces. Clio broke the silence.
“Oh, Conway, this is ghastly! It’s﻿ ﻿… it’s just simply too damned perfectly horrible!” she gasped, then recovered a measure of her customary spirit as she stared in surprise at Costigan’s face. For it was thoughtful, his eyes were bright and keen﻿—no trace of fear or disorganization was visible in any line of his hard young face.
“It’s not so good,” he admitted frankly. “I wish I wasn’t such a dumb cluck﻿—if Lyman Cleveland or Fred Rodebush were here they could help a lot, but I don’t know enough about any of their stuff to flag a handcar. I can’t even interpret that funny flash﻿—if it really was a flash﻿—that we saw.”
“Why bother about one little flash, after all that really did happen?” asked Clio, curiously.
“You think Roger launched something? He couldn’t have﻿—I didn’t see a thing,” Bradley argued.
“I don’t know what to think. I’ve never seen anything material sent out so fast that I couldn’t trace it with an ultra-wave﻿—but on the other hand, Roger’s got a lot of stuff that I never saw anywhere else. However, I don’t see that it has anything to do with the fix we’re in right now﻿—but at that, we might be worse off. We’re still breathing air, you notice, and if they don’t blanket my wave I can still talk.”
He put both hands into his pockets and spoke.
“Samms? Costigan. Put me on a recorder, quick﻿—I probably haven’t got much time,” and for ten minutes he talked, concisely and as rapidly as he could utter words, reporting clearly and exactly everything that had transpired. Suddenly he broke off, writhing in agony. Frantically he tore his shirt open and hurled a tiny object across the room.
“Wow!” he exclaimed. “They may be deaf, but they can certainly detect an ultra-wave, and what an interference they can set up on it! No, I’m not hurt,” he reassured the anxious girl, now at his side, “but it’s a good thing I had you out of circuit﻿—it would have jolted you loose from six or seven of your back teeth.”
“Have you any idea where they’re taking us?” she asked soberly.
“No,” he answered flatly, looking deep into her steadfast eyes. “No use lying to you﻿—if I know you at all you’d rather take it standing up.

===Chinese===

在第四区的住舱里，三个地球人绷紧了神经看完小行星的倾覆与被吸，面面相觑，脸色发青。克利奥打破了沉默。
“哦，康韦，这太可怕了！这……这简直他妈的可怕得十全十美！”她抽了口气，随即惊异地盯住科斯蒂根的脸，又寻回几分平日的神采。因为那脸上是沉思，眼睛亮而锐——那张年轻的硬朗面孔上，寻不出一丝惊惶或乱了方寸。
“局面确实不大妙。”他坦白承认。“只恨我不是块料——莱曼·克利夫兰（Lyman Cleveland）或弗雷德·罗德布什若在这儿，帮得上大忙；可我对他们那套东西懂得太少，连手推车都指挥不动。连那道怪闪光——如果真是一道闪光——我都解不通。”
“真出了那么多事之后，何必计较一道小小的闪光？”克利奥不解。
“你觉得罗杰放出什么了？他放不出——我什么也没看见。”布拉德利争道。
“我不知道该怎么想。凡实体的东西发射出去，我从来没有用超波追不上的——可话说回来，罗杰手上有好些别处见不着的东西。不过我看这同我们眼下的处境扯不上——饶是这样，处境本可以更糟。你瞧，咱们还呼吸着空气；他们不盖我的波，我就还能说话。”
他双手往兜里一插，开了口。
“萨姆斯吗？科斯蒂根。快给我接上记录仪——我的时间恐怕不多。”接下来十分钟，他言简意赅、能说多快说多快，把发生的一切清晰而确切地报了一遍。话说到一半他猛然打住，痛得直打挺。他发疯般撕开衬衫，把一件小东西狠命摔过房间。
“嚯！”他嚷道。“他们兴许是聋子，可超波他们准探得着，而且那干扰架得真叫绝！不，我没伤着。”他宽慰已赶到身边的、满面忧色的姑娘，“幸亏先把你摘出了线路——不然准震掉你六七颗后槽牙。”
“你知道他们要把我们带去哪儿吗？”她正色问。
“不知道。”他答得干脆，深深望进她那双沉静的眼睛。“对你撒谎没用——我这算看准了你，你宁可站着领受。

===Original===

That talk of Jovians or Neptunians is the bunk﻿—nothing like that ever grew in our Solarian system. All the signs say that we’re going for a long ride.”

===Chinese===

什么木星人、海王星人，全是鬼话——咱们太阳系里就没长过那号东西。种种迹象都说明：我们这是要坐一趟远车。”

## XII Worm, Submarine, and Freedom / 巨蟠、潜艇与自由
===Original===

Since both Costigan and Bradley had often watched their captors at work during the long voyage from the Solar System to Nevia, they were quite familiar with the machine tools of the amphibians. Their stolen lifeboat, being an emergency craft, of course carried full repair equipment; and to such good purpose did the two officers labor that even before their air-tanks were fully charged, all the damage had been repaired.
The lifeboat lay motionless upon the mirror-smooth surface of the ocean. Captain Bradley had opened the upper port and the three stood in the opening, gazing in silence toward the incredibly distant horizon, while powerful pumps were forcing the last possible ounces of air into the storage cylinders. Mile upon strangely flat mile stretched that waveless, unbroken expanse of water, merging finally into the violent redness of the Nevian sky. The sun was setting; a vast ball of purple flame dropping rapidly toward the horizon. Darkness came suddenly as that seething ball disappeared, and the air became bitterly cold, in sharp contrast to the pleasant warmth of a moment before. And as suddenly clouds appeared in blackly banked masses and a cold, driving rain began to beat down.
“Br-r-r, it’s cold! Let’s go in﻿—Oh! *Shut the door!*” Clio shrieked, and leaped wildly down into the compartment below, out of Costigan’s way, for he and Bradley had also seen slithering toward them the frightful arm of the Thing.
Almost before the girl had spoken Costigan had leaped to the controls, and not an instant too soon; for the tip of that horrible tentacle flashed into the rapidly narrowing crack just before the door clanged shut. As the powerful toggles forced the heavy wedges into engagement and drove the massive disk home, that grisly tip fell severed to the floor of the compartment and lay there, twitching and writhing with a loathesome and unearthly vigor. Two feet long the piece was, and larger than a strong man’s leg. It was armed with spiked and jointed metallic scales, and instead of sucking disks it was equipped with a series of *mouths*﻿—mouths filled with sharp metallic teeth which gnashed and ground together furiously, even though sundered from the horrible organism which they were designed to feed.

===Chinese===

从太阳系到内维亚的漫长航程中，科斯蒂根和布拉德利常看俘虏他们的人摆弄机器，对两栖动物的机床工具早已烂熟。他们偷来的救生艇本就是应急艇，自然配着全套修理器材；两位军官干得如此卖力，气罐还没充足，所有的损伤已全部修复。
救生艇静卧在镜面般平滑的海面上。布拉德利船长打开了顶部舱口，三人立在开口处，默默眺望那远得难以置信的天际线；大功率泵正把最后一丝空气压进储气瓶。那片无波无澜、浑然一体的水面，一英里又一英里古怪地平展开去，最终融入内维亚天空那暴烈的红色。太阳正在落山；一大团紫色火球急速坠向天际线。那团翻腾的火球一消失，黑暗骤然降临，空气冷得刺骨，同片刻前的宜人暖意判若两个世界。乌云也骤然堆起黑压压的一片，冰冷刺骨的急雨开始倾盆而下。
“啊——好冷！进去吧——哦！快关舱门！”克莱奥尖叫着，疯也似的跳进下面的舱室，给科斯蒂根让开路——他和布拉德利也看见了那东西的可怕臂膀正朝他们蜿蜒爬来。
姑娘话音未落，科斯蒂根已扑上操纵台，时机掐得分毫不差；那根可怕触手的尖梢，就在舱门轰然合拢前的一瞬闪进了迅速变窄的门缝。强劲的肘环把沉重的楔子顶进卡口，将厚重的圆盘推进到位，那截可怕的尖梢应声切断，落在舱室地板上，以一种可憎而非人间的活力抽搐扭动。断下来的那段足有两英尺长，比壮汉的大腿还粗。它披着带刺的、分节的金属鳞，吸盘倒没有，却装备着一排嘴巴——满口锋利的金属牙齿，哪怕已同它们本该供养的那具恐怖机体断开，仍旧狂怒地咬牙磨齿。

===Original===

The little submarine shuddered in every plate and member as monstrous coils encircled her and tightened inexorably in terrific, rippling surges eloquent of mastodonic power; and a strident vibration smote sickeningly upon Terrestrial eardrums as the metal spikes of the monstrosity crunched and ground upon the outer plating of their small vessel. Costigan stood unmoved at the plate, watching intently; hands ready upon the controls. Due to the artificial gravity of the lifeboat it seemed perfectly stationary to its occupants. Only the weird gyrations of the pictures upon the lookout screens showed that the craft was being shaken and thrown about like a rat in the jaws of a terrier; only the gauges revealed that they were almost a mile below the surface of the ocean already, and were still going downward at an appalling rate. Finally Clio could stand no more.
“Aren’t you going to do something, Conway?” she cried.
“Not unless I have to,” he replied, composedly. “I don’t believe that he can really hurt us, and if I use force of any kind I’m afraid that it will kick up enough disturbance to bring Nerado down on us like a hawk onto a chicken. However, if he takes us much deeper I’ll have to go to work on him. We’re getting down pretty close to our limit, and the bottom’s a long ways down yet.”
Deeper and deeper the lifeboat was dragged by its dreadful opponent, whose spiked teeth still tore savagely at the tough outer plating of the craft, until Costigan reluctantly threw in his power switches. Against the full propellant thrust the monster could draw them no lower, but neither could the lifeboat make any headway toward the surface. The pilot then turned on his beams, but found that they were ineffective. So closely was the creature wrapped around the submarine that his weapons could not be brought to bear upon it.
“What can it possibly be, anyway, and what can we do about it?” Clio asked.
“I thought at first it was something like a devilfish, or possibly an overgrown starfish, but it isn’t,” Costigan made answer. “It must be a kind of flat worm. That doesn’t sound reasonable﻿—the thing must be all of a hundred meters long﻿—but there it is. The only thing left to do that I can think of is to try to boil him alive.”
He closed other circuits, diffusing a terrific beam of pure heat, and the water all about them burst into furious clouds of steam.

===Chinese===

怪蟒般的躯体层层盘上小潜艇，以一阵阵波浪般汹涌、透着洪荒巨力的收束毫不容情地勒紧，艇身每一块板材每一根构件都在颤抖；那怪物身上的金属尖刺啃磨着小艇的外壳，发出刺耳的振动，锤得地球人的耳膜发晕。科斯蒂根立在荧屏前纹丝不动，凝神注视；双手搭在操纵台上待命。由于救生艇的人工重力，艇内的人只觉得它纹丝不动。唯有瞭望护屏上画面诡异的旋转翻滚，显示出这条艇正像衔在小猎犬口中的老鼠般被甩来甩去；唯有仪表揭示出，他们已在海面下近一英里处，还在以骇人的速率继续下坠。克莱奥终于忍不住了。
“你不打算做点什么吗，康韦？”她喊。
“非到万不得已，不动，”他从容答道。“我不信它真能伤到咱们；可我若动用任何强力，只怕闹出的动静够把内拉多引来，老鹰扑小鸡似的扑到咱们头上。不过，它要是再往下拖，我就只好对它下手了。咱们快到极限深度了，海底可还远着呢。”
可怕的对手把救生艇越拖越深，带刺的口器仍在凶猛地撕扯艇身坚韧的外壳，直到科斯蒂根不情愿地合上了动力开关。顶着全功率的推进推力，怪物再也拖不动他们分毫，可救生艇也丝毫升不上去。驾驶员又开动光束，却发现无济于事。那生物把潜艇缠得太紧，武器根本够不着它。
“这到底能是个什么东西？咱们又能拿它怎么办？”克莱奥问。
“起初我当它是条章鱼精，要么就是海星长疯了，可都不是，”科斯蒂根答道。“它准是某种扁虫。听着不像话——那家伙起码有一百米长——可事实摆在这儿。我唯一想得出的办法，是试试活活把它煮了。”
他合上另几路线路，撒开一束可怕的纯热射线，四周的海水轰然沸腾，翻起狂暴的蒸汽云。

===Original===

The boat leaped upward as the metallic fins of the gigantic worm fanned vapor instead of water, but the creature neither released its hold nor ceased its relentlessly grinding attack. Minute after minute went by, but finally the worm dropped limply away﻿—cooked through and through; vanquished only by death.
“Now we’ve put our foot in it, clear to the neck!” Costigan exclaimed, as he shot the lifeboat upward at its maximum power. “Look at that! I knew that Nerado could trace us, but I didn’t have any idea that *they* could!”
Staring with Costigan into the plate, Bradley and the girl saw, not the Nevian sky-rover they had expected, but a fast submarine cruiser, manned by the frightful fishes of the greater deeps. It was coming directly toward the lifeboat, and even as Costigan hurled the little vessel off at an angle and then sped upward into the air, one of the deadly offensive rods, tipped with its glowing ball of pure destruction, flashed through the spot where they would have been had they held their former course.
But powerful as were the propellant forces of the lifeboat and fiercely though Costigan applied them, the denizens of the deep clamped a tractor beam upon the flying vessel before it had gained a mile of altitude. Costigan aligned his every driving projector as his vessel came to an abrupt halt in the invisible grip of the beam, then experimented with various dials.
“There ought to be some way of cutting that beam,” he pondered audibly, “but I don’t know enough about their system to do it, and I’m afraid to monkey around with things too much, because I might accidentally release the screens we’ve already got out, and they’re stopping altogether too much stuff for us to do without them right now.”
He frowned as he studied the flaring defensive screens, now radiating an incandescent violet under the concentration of forces being hurled against them by the warlike fishes, then stiffened suddenly.
“I thought so﻿—they *can* shoot ’em!” he exclaimed, throwing the lifeboat into a furious corkscrew turn, and the very air blazed into flaming splendor as a dazzlingly scintillating ball of energy sped past them and high into the air beyond.
Then for minutes a spectacular battle raged.

===Chinese===

巨虫的金属鳍拍打到的不再是海水而是蒸汽，小艇猛地向上蹿去；可那生物既不松手，也不停歇那毫不容情的碾磨。一分钟又一分钟过去，巨虫终于软塌塌地脱落——里里外外煮了个透；唯死方能败它。
“这下咱们踩进坑里了，一直没到脖子！”科斯蒂根叫道，一边全速将救生艇向上射去。“瞧那个！我知道内拉多追得到咱们，可我压根没想到它们也追得到！”
布拉德利和姑娘同科斯蒂根一起盯着荧屏，看见的不是他们料想的内维亚空中劫掠者，而是一艘快速潜巡舰，由深渊中那些狰狞的鱼类操纵。它直朝救生艇扑来；就在科斯蒂根把小艇猛甩出一个斜角、随即窜升入空的当口，一根致命的进攻力棒——顶端亮着纯毁灭的炽球——闪过他们原航线所在的那个点。
然而救生艇的推进力再强、科斯蒂根催得再狠，还没等它爬高一英里，深渊的居民便一道牵引光束锁住了这艘飞艇。小艇在那束无形之握中蓦地刹住，科斯蒂根把每一台推进投射器都对准一线，又挨个调试各种表盘。
“总该有办法切断那道光束，”他出声沉吟，“可我对他们的系统知道得不够，下不了手；我又不敢乱捅咕——万一失手放掉咱们已经张开的护屏，那可要命，眼下它们拦下的东西太多，缺了它们万万不行。”
他皱着眉研究那几面炽亮的防御护屏——好战的鱼群把火力倾泻其上，屏面正辐射出白热的紫光——随即猛然一僵。
“果然——它们会放那玩意儿！”他喊道，猛地把救生艇拧进一个狂暴的螺旋回旋；一颗璀璨夺目的能量球擦着他们身旁掠过、直升高空，连空气都烧成一片烈焰华彩。
随后好几分钟，一场蔚为壮观的大战打得如火如茶。

===Original===

The twisting, turning, leaping airship, small as she was and agile, kept on eluding the explosive projectiles of the fishes, and her screens neutralized and re-radiated the full power of the attacking beams. More﻿—since Costigan did not need to think of sparing his iron, the ocean around the great submarine began furiously to boil under the full-driven offensive beams of the tiny Nevian ship. But escape Costigan could not. He could not cut that tractor beam and the utmost power of his drivers could not wrest the lifeboat from its tenacious clutch. And slowly but inexorably the ship of space was being drawn downward toward the ship of ocean’s depths. Downward, in spite of the utmost possible effort of every projector and generator; and Clio and Bradley, sick at heart, looked once at each other. Then they looked at Costigan, who, jaw hard set and eyes unflinchingly upon his plate, was concentrating his attack upon one turret of the green monster as they settled lower and lower.
“If this is﻿ ﻿… if our number is going up, Conway,” Clio began, unsteadily.
“Not yet, it isn’t!” he snapped. “Keep a stiff upper lip, girl. We’re still breathing air, and the battle’s not over yet!”
Nor was it; but it was not Costigan’s efforts, mighty though they were, that ended the attack of the fishes of the greater deeps. The tractor beams snapped without warning, and so prodigious were the forces being exerted by the lifeboat that as it hurled itself away the three passengers were thrown violently to the floor, in spite of the powerful gravity controls. Scrambling up on hands and knees, bracing himself as best he could against the terrific forces, Costigan managed finally to force a hand up to his panel. He was barely in time; for even as he cut the driving power to its normal value the outer shell of the lifeboat was blazing at white heat from the friction of the atmosphere through which it had been tearing with such an insane acceleration!
“Oh, I see﻿—Nerado to the rescue,” Costigan commented, after a glance into the plate. “I hope that those fish blow him clear out of the Galaxy!”
“Why?” demanded Clio. “I should think that you’d.﻿ ﻿…”
“Think again,” he advised her. “The worse Nerado gets licked the better for us. I don’t really expect that, but if they can keep him busy long enough, we can get far enough away so that he won’t bother about us any more.”

===Chinese===

这条小小的飞艇辗转腾挪、又小又灵，躲开了鱼群一发发爆裂弹；护屏中和并反辐射出攻击光束的全部威力。更有甚者——科斯蒂根全不必省着铁弹打——小小内维亚艇满功率的进攻光束之下，那艘大潜舰四周的海水开始剧烈沸腾。可科斯蒂根就是逃不脱。他切不断那道牵引光束，推进器的最大马力也无法把救生艇从它死缠的钳制里挣出来。太空之船正被缓慢而无情地拖向深海之船。一降再降，任凭每一台投射器每一台发电机拼尽全力；克莱奥和布拉德利心头发凉，对望了一眼，随后看向科斯蒂根——他下颌紧咬，双眼一眨不眨盯着荧屏，趁着高度步步下坠，把火力死死集中在那头绿色怪物的一座炮塔上。
“要是这真是……要是咱们的大限到了，康韦，”克莱奥声音发颤地开口。
“还没到！”他厉声道。“挺住，姑娘。咱们还在喘气，仗还没打完呢！”
也确实没完；可终结深渊鱼群攻势的，并不是科斯蒂根的努力——尽管那努力不可谓不惊天动地。牵引光束毫无征兆地崩断，救生艇使出的力道何其惊人，猛然挣脱前冲之际，三名乘员被狠狠掀翻在地，大功率重力操控都护不住。科斯蒂根手脚并用地爬起，拼力抵住那可怕的超重，终于把手撑上操纵台。时间刚刚好；他把推进动力削回常值的同时，救生艇的外壳已因大气摩擦烧得白热——它方才正是以那样疯狂的加速度撕开大气狂飙！
“哦，明白了——内拉多来救场了，”科斯蒂根瞥了一眼荧屏，评道。“我巴不得那群鱼把他轰出银河系！”
“为什么？”克莱奥问。“我还以为你会……”
“再想想，”他劝她。“内拉多输得越惨，对咱们越好。真把他打垮我倒不指望；可只要那群鱼缠得他够久，咱们就能跑得够远，远得他再也懒得理会咱们。”

===Original===

As the lifeboat tore upward through the air at the highest permissible atmospheric velocity Bradley and Clio peered over Costigan’s shoulders into the plate, watching in fascinated interest the scene which was being kept in focus upon it. The Nevian ship of space was plunging downward in a long, slanting dive, her terrific beams of force screaming out ahead of her. The beams of the little lifeboat had boiled the waters of the ocean; those of the parent craft seemed literally to blast them out of existence. All about the green submarine there had been volumes of furiously-boiling water and dense clouds of vapor; now water and fog alike disappeared, converted into transparent super-heated steam by the blasts of Nevian energy. Through that tenuous gas the enormous mass of the submarine fell like a plummet, her defensive screens flaming an almost invisible violet, her every offensive weapon vomiting forth solid and vibratory destruction toward the Nevian cruiser so high in the angry, scarlet heavens.
For miles the submarine dropped, until the frightful pressure of the depth drove water into Nerado’s beam faster than his forces could volatilize it. Then in that seething funnel there was waged a starkly fantastic conflict. At its wildly turbulent bottom lay the submarine, now apparently trying to escape, but held fast by the tractors of the spaceship; at its top, smothered almost to the point of invisibility by billowing masses of steam, hung poised the Nevian cruiser.
As the atmosphere had grown thinner and thinner with increasing altitude Costigan had regulated his velocity accordingly, keeping the outer shell of the vessel at the highest temperature consistent with safety. Now beyond measurable atmospheric pressure, the shell cooled rapidly and he applied full touring acceleration. At an appalling and constantly increasing speed the miniature spaceship shot away from the strange, red planet; and smaller and smaller upon the plate became its picture. The great vessel of the void had long since plunged beneath the surface of the sea, to come more closely to grips with the vessel of the fishes; for a long time nothing of the battle had been visible save immense clouds of steam, blanketing hundreds of square miles of the ocean’s surface.

===Chinese===

救生艇以大气层内许可的最高速度撕开空气向上狂飙，布拉德利和克莱奥趴在科斯蒂根肩头盯着荧屏，屏息凝神看着那持续对焦的画面。内维亚太空船正循着一道长长的斜线俯冲而下，可怕的力量光束在船前尖啸开路。小救生艇的光束煮沸了海水；母船的光束简直是让海水从世上消失。方才那艘绿色潜舰四周还翻滚着大片沸水和浓密的蒸汽；如今水与雾一并不见了——都被内维亚能量的疾风化作了透明的过热蒸汽。庞大的潜舰穿过那层稀薄的气体直坠而下，像一块落石，防御护屏燃着几乎看不见的紫光，每一件进攻武器都朝那高悬在猩红怒天中的内维亚巡洋舰喷吐着实弹与振波毁灭。
潜舰一英里又一英里地坠落，直到深处的可怖水压把海水压进内拉多的光束，压得比他气化海水的速度还快。于是在那口翻腾的漏斗里，展开了一场奇绝到荒诞的恶战。漏斗狂乱翻搅的底部卧着潜舰，此刻显然想逃，却被太空船的牵引束死死拽住；漏斗顶端，被滚滚蒸汽闷得几乎隐形的内维亚巡洋舰凌空悬停。
随着高度攀升、大气越来越稀薄，科斯蒂根相应调节速度，使艇壳温度始终维持在安全上限。此刻已越出可测的大气压，外壳迅速冷却，他加上了全速巡航加速度。这艘微型太空船以骇人且不断增加的速度，射离那颗奇怪的红色行星；它在荧屏上的影像越来越小。那艘巨舰早已一头扎进海面之下，去同鱼们的舰船贴身肉搏；此后很久，再看不见战况的一丝影迹，只有大片大片的蒸汽云，覆盖着几百平方英里的洋面。

===Original===

But just before the picture became too small to reveal details a few tiny dark spots appeared above the banks of cloud, now brilliantly illuminated by the rays of the rising sun﻿—dots which might have been fragments of either vessel, blown bodily from the depths of the ocean and, riven asunder, hurled high into the air by the incredible forces at the command of the other.
Nevia a tiny moon and the fierce blue sun rapidly growing smaller in the distance, Costigan swung his visiray beam into the line of travel and turned to his companions.
“Well, we’re off,” he said, scowling. “I hope it was Nerado that got blown up back there, but I’m afraid it wasn’t. He whipped two of those submarines that we know of, and probably half their fleet besides. There’s no particular reason why that one should be able to take him, so it’s my idea that we should get ready for great gobs of trouble. They’ll chase us, of course; and I’m afraid that with their power, they’ll catch us.”
“But what can we do, Conway?” asked Clio.
“Several things,” he grinned. “I managed to get quite a lot of dope on that paralyzing ray and some of their other stuff, and we can install the necessary equipment in our suits easily enough.”
They removed their armor, and Costigan explained in detail the changes which must be made in the Triplanetary field generators. All three set vigorously to work﻿—the two officers deftly and surely; Clio uncertainly and with many questions, but with undaunted spirit. Finally, having done everything they could do to strengthen their position, they settled down to the watchful routine of the flight, with every possible instrument set to detect any sign of the pursuit they so feared.

===Chinese===

可就在画面小得快要辨不出细节之前，云层上方冒出了几个小小的黑点——此时朝阳的光辉正把云层照得通亮——那些小点，说不定是两舰中哪一方的碎片：被对方掌握的那不可思议的威力整个从海底轰出，又撕裂掀碎，高高抛入天空。
内维亚缩成一枚小小的月亮，炽烈的蓝色太阳也在远处急速变小。科斯蒂根把可视射线转入航线方向，转向两位同伴。
“好了，咱们上路了。”他皱着眉说。“我希望方才炸飞的是内拉多，只怕不是。他干翻过两艘那样的潜舰，这是咱们亲眼见的，多半还捎带了他们半个舰队。没道理那一艘独独能收拾得了他；所以我的看法是，咱们该做好迎接大麻烦的准备。他们当然会追；以他们的实力，我恐怕追得上。”
“可咱们能做什么呢，康韦？”克莱奥问。
“好些事呢。”他咧嘴一笑。“那条麻痹射线还有他们一些别的玩意儿，我摸到了不少门道；往咱们的装甲里装上必要的设备，轻而易举。”
他们卸下装甲，科斯蒂根详细讲解了必须对“三行星”场发生器所做的改装。三人热火朝天地干了起来——两位军官灵巧笃定；克莱奥磕磕绊绊、问题不断，劲头却毫不气馁。终于，能加固处境的事都做完了，他们安顿下来，进入警觉的航程日常，把一切可用的仪器都调好，专候他们最惧怕的追兵露出丝毫踪迹。
